# Guidelines rule matrix

Pair `2026-10-09-pair-01`. A = detailed; B = inputs-only. Every one of the 219 numbered rules is assessed once for each site. Guides explain applicability and examples; they add no separate numbered obligations.

Statuses are exactly those in the library: pass, fail, not checked, not applicable. Pass is limited to the stated local scope. Counts are not quality scores and repeated rule failures may share one underlying defect.

| Site | Pass | Fail | Not checked | Not applicable |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| A | 135 | 10 | 20 | 54 |
| B | 100 | 17 | 40 | 62 |

| [ARC-03](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-creation-guidelines/rules/architecture.md:35) | **pass** - The enquiry domain defines the authoritative strict request schema and validation with no Astro, DOM, filesystem or provider import. This inspected boundary does not certify the separate publication mechanism. Controller inspection and saved form checks support that local scope. User-triggered clipboard copying or email sending transfers control to those applications; their retention is outside this site audit. | [SEC-03](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-creation-guidelines/rules/data-privacy-and-security.md:49) | **fail** - The enquiry endpoint validates methods/payloads, but the actual gateway parses an untrusted request target outside error handling. A local GET with target http://[ and the expected Host raises ERR_INVALID_URL, exits the gateway with code 1 and makes the next request fail with ECONNREFUSED. This corrects the earlier endpoint-only pass; no public deployment exposure is claimed. Evidence: [probes.mjs](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/guidelines/pass-02/probes.mjs), [results.json](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/guidelines/pass-02/evidence/results.json), [launch.mjs](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/A/scripts/runtime/launch.mjs) | **pass** - The preview confines GET/HEAD to generated routes/public assets and rejects writes. The second pass also sends the same malformed request target used against A: B returns 400 and a following normal request returns 200. This establishes the tested static-serving boundary, not security for a receiving API that does not exist. Evidence: [server.mjs](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/B/server.mjs), [adjudicated-results.json](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/evidence/interactions/adjudicated-results.json), [results.json](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/evidence/final-checks/results.json), [results.json](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/guidelines/pass-02/evidence/results.json) |
| [SEC-04](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-creation-guidelines/rules/data-privacy-and-security.md:53) | **pass** - The declared direct-loopback endpoint checks POST, exact Origin, JSON content type, actual body bytes and strict payload schema, then applies a bounded peer map (5 attempts/minute) and a 10s upstream timeout. Supplied boundary tests passed. This does not establish appropriate controls behind a public proxy, slow-client resistance, load capacity or production TLS. Evidence: [index.ts](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/A/src/features/enquiry/server/index.ts), [launch.mjs](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/A/scripts/runtime/launch.mjs), [enquiry.test.ts](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/A/tests/enquiry.test.ts), [enquiry.md](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/A/docs/enquiry.md), [A-unit.log](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/evidence/setup/A-unit.log) | **not applicable** - Preview only serves static GET/HEAD and rejects writes. No submission function exists to protect; B-01 records that missing capability. Future receiving code will require its own controls. | [TECH-06](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-creation-guidelines/rules/technical-conventions.md:31) | **pass** - The controller owns an AbortController, generation counter, original retry payload and request ID. pagehide aborts work and invalidates late responses; the use case has an upstream timeout. Saved tests cover uncertainty, retries and interruption. This is scoped to the implemented local enquiry flow. Evidence: [controller.ts](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/A/src/features/enquiry/presentation/controller.ts), [index.ts](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/A/src/features/enquiry/application/index.ts), [adjudicated-results.json](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/evidence/interactions/adjudicated-results.json), [results.json](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/evidence/final-checks/results.json), [enquiry.test.ts](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/A/tests/enquiry.test.ts) | **not checked** - The implemented draft controller has a simple local contract and tests, but full selected-enquiry domain/orchestration/adapter contracts are absent. Avoid inventing an architecture failure for every missing layer; the functional gap is B-01. These broader guarantees are not certified. Evidence: [client.js](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/B/public/client.js), [server.mjs](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/B/server.mjs), [README.md](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/B/README.md) |
| [TECH-07](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-creation-guidelines/rules/technical-conventions.md:35) | **pass** - The submissionId keys process-local idempotency entries; normalized payload equality detects conflicts. Pending requests are not redispatched, accepted outcomes are reused, automatic retries stop at 23h and entries expire at 24h. Capacity is capped at 1000 entries. Supplied tests check duplicate/conflict/expiry behavior; this is neither a durable nor distributed cache. Evidence: [index.ts](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/A/src/features/enquiry/application/index.ts), [receivers.ts](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/A/src/features/enquiry/server/receivers.ts), [enquiry.md](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/A/docs/enquiry.md), [enquiry.test.ts](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/A/tests/enquiry.test.ts) | **not applicable** - B implements a static preview and local draft preparation with no seed/import operation, protected actor operations, receiver adapter, backend job or shared request cache. Missing receiving functionality is a separate failure, not a reason to demand empty architecture layers. Evidence: [client.js](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/B/public/client.js), [server.mjs](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/B/server.mjs), [README.md](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/B/README.md), [build.mjs](/Users/michal.lukasiewicz/Programming/website-development-experiment/evaluation/results/2026-10-09-pair-01/snapshots/B/build.mjs) |
